Method: 1. 在特定过滤的土耳其语语料库上从零开始训练1.49亿参数模型；2. 采用两阶段课程学习：先进行因果语言建模，再进行掩码语言建模，转换发生在WSD调度的稳定阶段；3. 在共享前缀后将长上下文扩展和学习率衰减分为两个分支；4. 通过教师蒸馏和多信号对比微调生成嵌入模型；5. 开发50,048 token的专用分词器。

Result: 1. 在土耳其语MS MARCO检索上比纯MLM提升2.7-3.7倍；2. TrGLUE得分78.41，优于对比的土耳其语ModernBERT模型；3. TabiBench得分77.73，在代码检索类别领先3.62分；4. MoganBert-Embed在MTEB(土耳其语)整体平均得分68.30，达到教师模型99.5%的性能但参数量减少51倍；5. 分词器在压缩和词汇丰富度方面优于所有对比的土耳其语分词器。

Conclusion: MoganBert-TR通过创新的训练策略和专用语料库处理，在土耳其语自然语言处理任务中实现了最先进的性能，同时其衍生的嵌入模型展现了高效的压缩能力，为土耳其语AI应用提供了强大的基础模型。

Abstract: Turkish encoder models have adopted modern architectures while leaving the pretraining objective fixed at masked language modelling. This paper introduces MoganBert-TR, a 149M-parameter Turkish encoder foundation model trained from scratch on a language-specifically filtered corpus, together with an embedding model derived from it (MoganBert-Embed).
  MoganBert-TR is trained over 237.3B tokens with a two-stage CLM-to-MLM curriculum: causal language modelling first, masked language modelling for the remainder, with the transition made inside the stable phase of a WSD schedule. In a controlled ablation under an equal step budget, this design outperforms pure MLM by 2.7-3.7x on Turkish MS MARCO retrieval; the measured mechanism is embedding geometry, where a single direction absorbs 28.1% of the variance under pure MLM against 11.9% under the curriculum. Long-context extension and learning-rate decay are then split into two branches after a shared prefix: running the final portion of decay at 1024 context improves the TrGLUE average by 0.49 +/- 0.26 points across five paired seeds (p = 0.013) and beats a model-soup alternative by 0.75 points at ~4.3% additional cost.
  MoganBert-TR attains 78.41 on TrGLUE, the best among the Turkish ModernBERT models compared, and 77.73 on TabiBench, where it leads two of the eight categories with the largest margin on code retrieval (+3.62 points over TabiBERT). MoganBert-Embed, produced through teacher distillation and multi-signal contrastive fine-tuning, ranks first among student models on the MTEB(Turkish) overall average with 68.30 and reaches 99.5% of its 7.57B-parameter teacher's score with a 51x smaller backbone. The accompanying 50,048-token tokenizer outperforms all compared Turkish tokenizers on compression and fertility across two independent test sets. Weights, tokenizer, embedding model and evaluation code: https://huggingface.co/moganai

</details>


### [142] [Localize-Then-Decide Guarantees for LLM Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25824)
*Xinyu Li,Yi Zhou,Guanqun Cao,Zeyu Fu,Tianjin Huang,Gaojie Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Localize-Then-Decide框架，用于解决大型语言模型作为评估者在多候选响应场景下置信度估计失真的问题。通过先使用符合性预测缩小候选范围，再基于校准置信度规则进行选择性选择或放弃，恢复置信度与人类分歧风险之间的单调关系，实现了高概率的同意保证。实验表明该方法在多种数据集和评估模型上均优于单阶段基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型日益被用作评估器来评估输出质量和偏好对齐，但提供与人类判断一致性的可靠保证仍然具有挑战性。现有基于置信度阈值的方法依赖于"高置信度意味着低分歧风险"的假设，但当候选响应数量增加时，由于概率质量分布到多个替代项会扭曲置信度估计，这一假设可能失效。

Method: 提出Localize-Then-Decide框架，包含两个阶段：1）利用符合性预测定位一个小型短名单，该短名单以高概率包含人类偏好的响应；2）使用校准的基于置信度的规则从短名单中选择单个响应或放弃选择。这种设计恢复了置信度与分歧风险之间的单调关系。

Result: 在多个候选规模、多个数据集和多个评判大型语言模型上的实验表明，所提框架一致实现了更高的保证成功率，并且覆盖率比单阶段基线方法显著提高。

Conclusion: Localize-Then-Decide框架通过两阶段处理有效解决了多候选响应场景下的置信度估计失真问题，恢复了置信度与人类分歧风险之间的单调关系，实现了高概率的同意保证，为大型语言模型作为评估器提供了更可靠的置信度保证机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as evaluators to assess output quality and preference alignment, yet providing reliable guarantees of agreement with human judgments remains challenging. Recent work introduces confidence-thresholding methods that provide such guarantees for pairwise comparisons, relying on the assumption that higher estimated confidence implies lower disagreement risk with humans. However, this assumption can break down when the number of candidate responses increases, since distributing probability mass across many alternatives can distort confidence estimates. To address this issue, we propose a Localize-Then-Decide framework. First, conformal prediction localizes a small shortlist that contains the human-preferred response with high probability. Then, a calibrated confidence-based rule selectively chooses a single response from this shortlist or abstains. This design restores the monotonic relationship between confidence and disagreement risk and enables high-probability agreement guarantees. Experiments with multiple candidate sizes across several datasets and judge LLMs demonstrate that our framework consistently achieves higher guarantee success rates and substantially higher coverage than single-stage baselines.

</details>


### [143] [Unfolding Scientific Papers into Multi-Turn Generation Trajectories for Continued Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25826)
*Qiankai Xu,Qiguang Chen,Zixin Su,Wenhao Huang,Yue Gao,Jiaheng Liu,Ge Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将科学论文重构为多轮生成轨迹的流水线，生成用于持续预训练的语料库，显著提升学术写作能力同时保持通用推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据工作仅处理短文本段落，恢复局部思维过程，未触及完整文档结构。科学论文具有清晰且统一的固定结构，适合将这一范式提升到文档级别。

Method: 构建流水线将每篇论文展开为多轮生成轨迹：教师模型重构整篇论文的写作过程，包括写作请求、全局计划和各章节的写作前思考。保留所有章节文本和摘要原文。应用于经过质量筛选的arXiv论文，生成约两倍于源文本规模的CPT语料库。同时扩展至指令数据和评估基准（PAW-Bench），以真实论文文本作为答案生成SFT数据集。

Result: 在受控实验中，在CPT语料库上预训练后在公共数据集上进行监督微调，广泛提升了写作基准测试成绩，同时保持通用推理能力并改善长文档阅读理解。即使所有模型都在专用写作SFT数据集上微调，写作提升效果仍然持续。将本文SFT数据混合到该方案中进一步提升了学术写作能力。

Conclusion: 成功将合成数据重构范式从短文本提升到完整科学文档级别，利用论文的结构化特性创建高质量预训练和微调资源，显著增强AI模型的学术写作能力。

Abstract: A recent line of synthetic-data work reconstructs the thinking behind existing text rather than rewriting the text itself, but it operates on short web passages, recovers only local thoughts, and leaves the structure of whole documents untouched. Scientific papers are written to a clear and largely uniform structure and make a natural substrate for lifting this paradigm to the document level. We present a pipeline that unfolds each paper into a multi-turn generation trajectory in which a teacher model reconstructs the writing process of the whole paper: a writing request, a global plan, and pre-writing deliberation for each section. All section texts and the abstract are kept verbatim from the source paper. We apply the pipeline to quality-filtered arXiv papers and obtain a corpus for continued pre-training (CPT) that is roughly twice the size of the source text. The same reverse construction extends to instruction data and evaluation. Treating real paper text as the answer yields an SFT dataset. Anchoring tasks in held-out papers yields PAW-Bench, an academic-writing benchmark whose tasks carry their own rubrics and checklists. In controlled experiments CPT on our corpus followed by supervised fine-tuning on public datasets improves writing benchmarks broadly while preserving general reasoning and improving long-document reading. The writing gain persists even when every model is fine-tuned on a dedicated writing SFT dataset. Mixing our SFT data into that recipe lifts academic writing further.

</details>


### [144] [Skill Issue: Are Skills Language-Invariant in LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25832)
*Bobby Cheng,Adam Gaber,Zhengyuan Liu,Catherine Arnett,Omer Goldman,Cheston Tan,Leshem Choshen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在不同语言下表现出显著的技能差异，这种跨语言技能不一致性是构建真正多语言模型的主要障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知大型语言模型在不同语言间访问知识的方式不一致，但它们在交互不同语言时技能集的差异程度尚不清楚，需要量化这种跨语言技能不一致性。

Method: 采用多语言自我博弈方法，让同一模型的两个实例通过不同语言接口参与文本游戏，隔离语言对行为的影响。构建了多语言版TextArena，在8种语言和6种游戏（涵盖空间推理、不完全信息、资源分配和重复交互）中评估了3个开放权重模型。

Result: 发现同一模型在不同语言下表现出显著不同的游戏强度，胜负差、无效行动和战略倾向存在系统性差异。详细分析揭示了在空间推理、基于卡牌的决策和最优移动选择方面的语言特定失败。在某些情况下，仅改变中间推理语言就能恢复大部分丢失的性能。

Conclusion: 技能差异是可测量的重大障碍，阻碍了真正多语言模型的发展。更好地理解这些差异有助于设计在跨语言间表现更公平的系统。

Abstract: Large language models access knowledge inconsistently across languages, but to what extent do they differ in their skill sets when interacting with different languages? This work quantifies cross-lingual skill inconsistency orthogonally from knowledge and general benchmark performance. We do this via multilingual self-play: two instances of the same model compete in a text-based game, each interacting through a different language interface. Since the model, opponent, rules, state space, and available actions remain fixed, this setting isolates the effect of language on the model's realized behavior. We build a multilingual extension to TextArena and evaluate three open-weight models across eight languages and six games covering spatial reasoning, imperfect information, resource allocation, and repeated interaction. We find that the same model can exhibit markedly different playing strength across languages, with systematic variation in win--loss margins, invalid actions, and strategic tendencies. Detailed analyses reveal language-specific failures in spatial reasoning, card-conditioned decisions, and optimal move selection. In some settings, changing only the intermediate reasoning language recovers much of the lost performance, suggesting that language can affect different stages of the decision process. These results show that skill discrepancies are a measurable major roadblock in the development of truly multilingual models. Better understanding these discrepancies can help us design models that perform more equitably across languages.

</details>


### [145] [Key Point Analysis Needs Structure Recovery: Task Definition, Dataset Diagnosis, and a Structure-Aware Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25854)
*Zhiqiang Shi,Oana Cocarascu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的关键点分析(KPA)基准测试方法，通过人工参与的重新标注解决了现有基准在分组质量、冗余、覆盖度和论证-关键点映射方面的局限性，实验表明新方法在分组连贯性、关键点质量、覆盖度和流行度估计方面均优于现有标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KPA基准测试存在分组质量差、冗余度高、覆盖不足以及论证-关键点映射不准确等根本性问题，导致基于参考的评估方法出现天花板违规和选择失败现象，需要建立更可靠的评估框架。

Method: 提出结构感知、分布敏感的KPA基准测试框架，采用人工参与的迭代重新标注策略构建高质量标注数据，通过人类评估和LLM评估双重验证标注质量。

Result: 实验结果表明，新方法产生的结构在分组连贯性、关键点质量、论证覆盖度和流行度估计可靠性方面均显著优于现有标注；同时发布了多个标注资源支持KPA评估、论证-关键点匹配、可解释KPA和LLM作为评判者的研究方法。

Conclusion: 真正的KPA需要结构化的预测框架，本文提供的改进基准和研究资源将推动KPA领域在评估方法、匹配技术和可解释性等方面的深入研究，为未来KPA研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Key Point Analysis (KPA) aims to identify a concise set of key points that summarize a collection of arguments together with their prevalence. We argue that KPA is fundamentally a structured prediction problem that requires recovering semantic groupings, generating representative key points, ensuring coverage, and estimating prevalence. Under this formulation, we show that existing KPA benchmarks suffer from limitations in grouping quality, redundancy, coverage, and argument-key point mappings, causing ceiling violation and selection failure in reference-based evaluation. To support future research on true KPA, we introduce a structure-aware, distribution-sensitive benchmark built via a human-in-the-loop re-annotation. Human and LLM evaluations consistently show that the resulting structures yield more coherent groupings, higher-quality key points, better coverage, and more reliable prevalence estimates than existing annotations. We further release several annotation resources to support research on KPA evaluation, argument-key point matching, explainable KPA, and LLM-as-a-judge methodologies, and outline a research agenda for true KPA.

</details>


### [146] [Anchoring Bias in LLM-as-a-Judge Systems: Prior Scores Compromise Evaluation Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25869)
*Ante Kapetanovic,Kemal Altwlkany,Andro Mercep,Tomislav Duricic,Emanuel Lacic*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了LLM-as-a-Judge范式中存在的锚定偏差现象：历史评分作为元数据上下文会系统性地影响后续评判，7/8个模型表现出显著偏差效应，偏差可阻断48%的错误修正并翻转10.18%的正确判断，表明可靠的LLM评估需要精心设计的上下文工程而非对公正性的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge系统在生产环境中越来越多地用于评估生成内容，但现有假设认为每次判断独立于先前评估，该假设尚未得到验证。

Method: 设计三种提示条件进行实验测试：无元数据、修订框架和包含修订、尝试次数、历史评分字段的锚定元数据。在192,000次评估尝试（185,271次成功）中，使用8个模型对20个固定文本进行测试，并在带有人工标注真值的基础类别行业数据上验证，同时进行词元级别分析并测试思维链和元数据忽略警告等缓解策略。

Result: 7/8模型在20个固定文本上锚定元数据总效应的95%任务分层引导区间低于零；Cohen's d标准化差异绝对值达0.71；词元级分析显示阈值样响应模式；锚定元数据阻断48%错误修正并翻转10.18%正确判断；思维链和元数据忽略警告未能减少总效应，但警告改善了行业实验中的配对准确性。

Conclusion: 历史评分即使仅作为上下文元数据也会锚定判断并系统性地使评分偏向其值，该偏差从数值评分延伸到类别决策；有效缓解必须针对特定模型和任务/领域进行验证，可靠LLM评估需要精心上下文工程而非公正性假设。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly assess generated content, giving rise to the LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm. These systems now score outputs, filter content, and gate iterative refinement in production pipelines, where each judgment is often assumed to be independent of earlier evaluations. We test this assumption using three prompt conditions: no metadata, revision framing, and anchored metadata containing revision, attempt, and prior-score fields. We show that prior scores, even when included only as context metadata, anchor judgments and systematically shift ratings toward their values. Across 192,000 attempted evaluations (185,271 successful), seven out of the eight evaluated models have 95% task-stratified bootstrap intervals below zero for the total anchored-metadata effect on 20 fixed texts. Cohen's $d$, a standardized measure of the difference between score distributions, reaches an absolute value of 0.71. Token-level analysis of selected model-task probes suggests a threshold-like response pattern: introducing anchored metadata produces a marked redistribution of output-score probabilities, while changing the anchor value within the tested below-threshold range produces comparatively little additional variation. On categorical industry data with human-labeled ground truth, anchored metadata blocks 48% of error corrections and flips 10.18% of correct judgments toward an assigned wrong label, demonstrating the bias extends beyond numerical scoring to categorical decisions. Neither Chain-of-Thought nor a metadata-disregard warning reduces the total effect, although the warning improves the paired accuracy effect relative to baseline in the industry experiment. Reliable LLM evaluation demands careful context engineering rather than an assumption of impartiality. Effective mitigation must be validated for the intended model and task or domain.

</details>


### [147] [Loss-Based Active Learning for Neural Abstractive Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25881)
*Michail Ioannou,Tatiana Passali,George Michalopoulos,Grigorios Tsoumakas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LOBSTER（基于损失的主动学习框架），通过优先选择与模型高损失训练样本语义相似的数据进行标注，有效解决了摘要模型微调中高质量标注数据获取困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抽取式摘要模型微调需要高质量标注数据，但人工标注耗时耗力。主动学习虽能减少标注量，但在摘要任务中存在不稳定性和计算瓶颈问题，亟需更高效的主动学习框架。

Method: LOBSTER框架通过识别模型当前高损失训练样本，选择与其语义最相似但未标注的实例进行优先标注，使模型能够针对性地纠正自身弱点，提高学习效率。

Result: 在三个基准数据集和两种摘要骨干模型上的实验表明，LOBSTER持续达到或超越现有最先进方法性能，同时查询选择速度提升高达665倍。

Conclusion: LOBSTER通过基于损失和语义相似性的主动学习策略，有效解决了摘要任务中标注数据稀缺的问题，为高效微调摘要模型提供了新方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning abstractive summarization models requires high-quality annotated data. However, obtaining such corpora is expensive and time-consuming, as it requires human annotators to read and comprehend long documents to create accurate summaries. Active learning mitigates this issue by selecting only the most informative instances for annotation, allowing models to achieve competitive results with significantly fewer labels. However, the application of active learning to summarization remains under-explored, and existing studies often suffer from instability and significant computational bottlenecks. To overcome these challenges, we propose LOBSTER (LOss-BaSed acTivE leaRning), a novel active learning framework designed specifically for abstractive summarization. LOBSTER improves performance by prioritizing unlabeled instances semantically similar to the model's current high-loss training examples, enabling the model to explicitly correct its specific weaknesses. Our empirical evaluation across three benchmark datasets and two summarization backbone models demonstrates that LOBSTER consistently matches or outperforms current state-of-the-art approaches while achieving a query selection speedup of up to 665x.

</details>


### [148] [From Passive Response to Proactive Correction: Enhancing LLM Robustness Against Input Fact Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25894)
*Ping Wang,Xiangguo Sun,Bingbing Xu,Guocong Li,Xiaofeng Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出DEDUCE框架，通过三阶段流程（错误检测→修正策略→纠正输出）帮助LLMs识别并纠正用户输入中的事实错误，显著提升模型鲁棒性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在用户输入包含误导性前提时，会生成自信但事实错误的回答。现有幻觉缓解方法假设用户输入可靠，忽视了事实错误对模型推理的主动误导作用。

Method: DEDUCE三阶段框架：(1)通过细粒度事实提取和验证检测错误；(2)通过多视角审慎制定修正策略；(3)纠正误解同时提供可靠答案。另构建MisFactQA数据集（含不同程度事实错误）并设计新评估指标。

Result: 在TruthfulQA、FalseQA和MisFactQA基准测试中，DEDUCE显著提升准确性和错误纠正能力。在Qwen、LLaMA和Gemma系列模型上均表现一致增益，证明其有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: DEDUCE成功将LLMs从被动应答者转变为主动错误纠正器，增强了模型对事实性错误输入的鲁棒性，为缓解幻觉问题提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently produce confident yet factually incorrect responses when user inputs contain misleading premises, a phenomenon we attribute to fact perturbations in the input. Existing approaches to hallucination mitigation typically assume reliable user inputs, overlooking how such factual errors can actively mislead model reasoning. To address this vulnerability, we propose DEDUCE, a three-stage framework that transforms LLMs from passive responders into proactive error correctors. DEDUCE operates in three stages: (1) detect errors through fine-grained fact extraction and verification; (2) devise correction strategies via multi perspective deliberation; and (3) correct misconceptions while delivering reliable answers. We also present MisFactQA, a dataset containing factual errors of varying degrees, and propose new metrics for evaluating model robustness. Experiments on TruthfulQA, FalseQA, and our MisFactQA benchmark demonstrate that DEDUCE significantly improves both accuracy and error correction capability. Consistent gains across Qwen, LLaMA, and Gemma families confirm its effectiveness and scalability.

</details>


### [149] [One Form to Transfer Them All: Pretraining Multilingual Language Models Beyond Native Orthography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25904)
*Muge Zhang,Aaron Jencks,Krishna Badikela,Yulia Tsvetkov,Sachin Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在多语言自回归预训练中，使用罗马化输入进行预训练能获得最强的跨语言迁移能力，且随着模型规模增大优势更明显；罗马化应作为核心设计选择应用于预训练阶段，而非事后修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言语言模型通过共享子词词汇实现跨语言知识迁移，但当相关语言使用不同书写系统时该机制失效。现有工作通过脚本均衡（罗马化或IPA转写）解决此问题，但直接比较罕见，且主要关注编码器模型并适配现有预训练模型，缺乏对自回归多语言预训练中不同输入表示的系统比较。

Method: 在受控设置下，系统比较正交文本、IPA和罗马化三种输入表示在自回归多语言预训练中的效果，使用三种模型规模（467M、709M、1.03B），涵盖四种类型学动机配对中的八种语言，并在已见和未见语言的广泛下游任务上评估。

Result: 罗马化预训练在跨语言迁移方面表现最强，且随模型规模增大相对于文本的优势更显著。IPA在大多数设置中优于文本但逊于罗马化。令人惊讶的是，在文本预训练模型上用罗马化数据微调会损害基础模型已覆盖语言的性能，仅在模型缺乏脚本覆盖时边际改善。

Conclusion: 对于跨越类型学多样脚本的多语言模型，为获得最大效益，罗马化应作为核心设计选择应用于预训练阶段，而非事后修正。

Abstract: Multilingual language models transfer knowledge across languages through shared subword vocabulary, a mechanism that breaks down when related languages use different writing systems. Prior work addresses this via script equalization (romanization or IPA transcription), but direct comparisons are rare; the focus has been on encoder-only models, with most work adapting existing pretrained models. We systematically compare different input representations in autoregressive multilingual pretraining, comparing orthographic text, IPA, and romanization in a controlled setup across three scales (467M, 709M, and 1.03B) on eight languages in four typologically motivated pairs. Across a wide range of downstream tasks on seen and unseen languages, romanized pretraining yields the strongest cross-lingual transfer, and the advantage over text widens with scale. IPA improves over text in most settings but trails romanization. Surprisingly, finetuning a text-pretrained model on romanized data hurts performance on languages already covered by the base model, only marginally helping when the model lacks script coverage. Our results indicate that for multilingual models spanning typologically diverse scripts, to obtain maximum benefits, romanization should be treated as a core design choice applied at pretraining rather than a post hoc fix.

</details>


### [150] [SAMpLE: A SystemC-AMS Machine LEarning-based Framework for Virtual Prototyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25910)
*Andrei Mihai Albu,Sara Vinco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SAMpLE，一个基于SystemC-AMS的开源框架，通过标准化的即插即用接口将机器学习模型作为一等时序数据流（TDF）组件集成到虚拟平台仿真中，解决了ML模型集成过程中缺乏标准化、复用性和可复现性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在嵌入式系统虚拟原型建模中的应用日益增加，但目前将ML模型集成到虚拟平台仿真中通常采用临时性解决方案，这限制了模型的复用性、可比较性和可复现性。

Method: SAMpLE框架采用基于SystemC-AMS的架构，将ML模型作为一等TDF组件集成。提供两个执行后端：原生C++后端用于轻量级模型的在线训练，以及离线后端用于执行外部开发的模型。框架使用ONNX作为标准模型交换格式，实现外部训练ML模型与SystemC-AMS仿真的集成。

Result: 该框架允许在同一测试台、数据集和仿真工作流中评估不同的ML解决方案，实现了ML模型在SystemC-AMS仿真环境中的标准化集成，无需重新实现C++代码或手动集成步骤。

Conclusion: SAMpLE的模块化设计和统一的可复现环境，使得未来可以扩展新模型而无需修改SystemC-AMS结构，为ML模型在嵌入式系统虚拟原型中的应用提供了可扩展的基础设施。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML) is increasingly used in virtual prototypes of embedded systems to model behaviors that are difficult to capture analytically. However, integrating ML models into virtual platform simulation is still typically done through ad hoc solutions, which limits reuse, comparability, and reproducibility. This paper presents \textbf{\textit{SAMpLE}}, an open-source SystemC-AMS-based framework that integrates ML models as first-class Timed Dataflow (TDF) components through a standardized plug-and-play interface. SAMpLE provides two execution backends: a native C++ backend for online training of lightweight models, and an offline backend for executing externally developed models without requiring re-implementation in C++ or manual integration steps. The framework uses ONNX as a standard model exchange format to enable integration of externally trained ML models into SystemC-AMS simulations, and allows the evaluation of different ML-based solutions within the same testbench, dataset, and simulation workflow. The modular design and unified and reproducible environment will allow future extensions of SAMpLE to new models, without modifying the SystemC-AMS structure.

</details>


### [151] [Query-Side Attacks on GNN-Based KGQA: Tracing Failures from Entity Linking to Answer Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25922)
*Pankaj Kumar,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入阶段隔离协议，发现GNN知识库问答系统中99%以上的性能崩溃源于子图构建阶段（而非GNN推理阶段），即使正确答案存在但无法被有效检索时也会失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端鲁棒性评估将各阶段失败混淆为单一指标，导致无法识别脆弱性来源及明确优化目标。需精确定位对抗扰动下失败的具体阶段及其成因。

Method: 设计两种答案保持型对抗扰动：1) 组合重构(CR)干扰子图检索逻辑 2) 关系同义词替换(RS)影响实体链接过程。在ComplexWebQuestions/WebQSP数据集上验证并分离各阶段贡献。

Result: CR扰动下GNN推理阶段保持接近基线准确率；子图构建错误占端到端崩溃的99%，且74%情况下正确答案存在于检索子图却不可达。证明存在'答案存在性'与'答案可达性'的本质差异。

Conclusion: 核心瓶颈在于子图构建阶段的检索能力而非推理模型本身，应将优化资源集中于改进子图生成算法。发布了对应扰动数据集与评估工具包。

Abstract: GNN-based Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA) pipelines process queries through four discrete stages: entity linking, subgraph retrieval, GNN reasoning, and answer generation. Standard robustness evaluations conflate stage-level failures into a single end-to-end metric, obscuring both the source of brittleness and the appropriate mitigation target. We ask which stage fails, and why, when the pipeline is subjected to adversarial perturbations on the input question. We introduce a stage-isolation protocol with two answer-preserving adversarial perturbations verified against the knowledge graph: Compositional Restructuring (CR) and Relation Synonym Swap (RS) target distinct stages while leaving entity seeds intact. Evaluated across ComplexWebQuestions and WebQSP, the results run counter to prevailing assumptions: the GNN reasoning stage retains near-baseline accuracy when the subgraph is intact, while subgraph construction accounts for over 99\% of the end-to-end collapse under CR, occurring even when the gold answer is present in 74\% of retrieved subgraphs. This exposes a fundamental distinction between answer presence and answer reachability that end-to-end metrics cannot detect, and places the mitigation target firmly at the subgraph construction stage rather than the reasoning model. Perturbed datasets and evaluation infrastructure are released at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/atkgrag-E85C .

</details>


### [152] [Unveiling Spectral Mechanisms in Training-Free LLM Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25944)
*Haitong Luo,Xuying Meng,Weiyao Zhang,Wenji Zou,Shengfeng Lou,Xuefeng Jiang,Chungang Lin,Yujun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析频谱检测AI生成文本的机制与边界，发现其通过捕捉人类写作中的"生成活力"（信号波动）来区分人机文本，检测效果受文本长度与采样范围影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型快速发展使人机文本难以区分；现有基于置信度的检测方法仅测量平均token概率，忽略了人类写作特有的信号波动；频谱分析虽能捕捉这种"生成活力"，但其机制与实际应用边界尚未充分探索。

Method: 从理论与实证两个角度分析频谱检测：将频谱能量与代理对数概率轨迹的方差相关联；解释人类更广泛的token选择如何产生频域指标可利用的波动；在不同文本长度与采样范围内测试信号强度。

Result: 频谱能量与对数概率轨迹方差相关；人类更广泛的token选择创造了可检测的波动信号；信号强度取决于文本长度与采样范围——长、连续、受限生成场景下频谱证据最清晰，短、碎片化、混合、编辑场景需要互补的置信度与波动视角。

Conclusion: 研究明确了频域检测的适用场景，为未来多维度检测器设计提供指导，建议结合频谱、置信度与波动视角进行综合检测。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) makes it increasingly difficult to distinguish human writing from machine-generated text. Training-free detection offers a scalable solution, yet common confidence-based metrics mainly measure average token probabilities and often miss the signal fluctuations that characterize human writing, which we call "generative vitality". Spectral analysis offers a way to capture this vitality, but its mechanism and practical boundaries remain underexplored. In this paper, we analyze spectral detection from both theoretical and empirical perspectives. We connect spectral energy to variance in proxy log-probability trajectories and explain how broader human token choices create the fluctuations used by frequency-domain indicators. We further show that the strength of this signal depends on text length and sampling range: spectral evidence is clearest for long, continuous, constrained generation, while short, fragmented, mixed, and edited settings require complementary confidence and fluctuation views. These findings clarify when frequency-domain detection works and provide guidance for future multi-dimensional detector design.

</details>


### [153] [Lost but not erased: Finding traces of a forgotten language in neural speech models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25976)
*Peter Plantinga,Charlotte Moore,Peter W. Donhauser,Krista Byers-Heinlein,Denise Klein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 国际收养者保留母语语音痕迹的现象可能源于学习动态而非生物学关键期，自动语音识别模型实验证实早期语言经验会形成持久性底层表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统将语言习得关键期效应归因于生物成熟度的观点，探究国际收养者保留母语语音痕迹是否由普通学习动态而非发育时程决定。

Method: 使用自动语音识别模型模拟收养者语言转换经验，训练模型先接触第一语言后突然切换至第二语言，排除生物成熟变量干扰，观察语言表征在模型各层级的变化及后续语言学习效率。

Result: 第一语言痕迹持续存在于最低层的前音素层；早期接触模型比无经验模型快14%重新习得丧失的母语，该优势仅依赖于最早层特征，替换这些层即消除优势。

Conclusion: 关键期效应反映的是基础表征的固化而非可塑性丧失，经验在语言习得关键期中起核心作用，支持学习动态解释框架。

Abstract: International adoptees retain phonological traces of a birth language they can no longer speak or comprehend, a persistence typically attributed to a biologically-timed critical period. We asked whether it could instead reflect the ordinary dynamics of learning, using automatic speech recognition models that simulate the international adoptee experience without maturational confounds. Models were trained on one language and then abruptly switched to a second. We found that traces of the first language persisted throughout second-language training, but mainly in the lowest, pre-phonemic layers. These traces were functional, as models with early exposure re-learned their lost first language 14% faster than naive models; this advantage held even against models adopted early from a related language and disappeared when the earliest layers were substituted from a non-adopted model. We argue that these critical-period effects reflect entrenchment of foundational representations rather than a maturational loss of plasticity, and that experience plays a central role in critical periods in language acquisition.

</details>


### [154] [When Personality Meets Quantization: A Layer-wise MBTI Analysis of Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25977)
*Yao Fu,Lijia Huang,Xiaomin Li,Runchao Li,Yu Yin,Kenneth A. Loparo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了多精度量化LLM的MBTI人格特征，揭示了人格的动态涌现性及其对量化、推理策略的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM人格影响用户信任与体验，但现有MBTI研究多局限于全精度模型及最终输出，忽视了广泛应用的量化模型的人格特征，以及人格在模型内部层间的动态演变机制。

Method: 对开源LLM在不同量化精度（4-bit GPTQ/AWQ, 2-bit AQLM）下进行MBTI分析。通过选项级熵和置信度差分析层间人格涌现过程，并引入不确定性放大层解码（UALD）研究推理阶段的人格漂移现象。

Result: 发现ENFJ人格在各类模型中占主导；4-bit量化能保留粗粒度人格结构，而2-bit量化会破坏提示一致性和跨精度一致性；人格决策在高层网络中涌现，早期层存在大量歧义；推理解码过程会改变人格，但人格对齐的条件化可增强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LLM人格并非静态属性，而是依赖层级且对量化和推理敏感的动态决策过程。研究为量化LLM的行为可靠性提供了新视角，强调了在敏感聊天机器人应用中关注内部动态和推理策略的重要性。

Abstract: Personality is increasingly important in large language models (LLMs), as it shapes users' trust, engagement, and emotional experiences. While the Myers--Briggs Type Indicator (MBTI) has emerged as a common framework for assessing LLMs' personality, existing studies focus primarily on full-precision models and evaluate only final outputs. They overlook the widespread deployment of quantized LLMs requiring low memory footprints, whose personality traits remain underexplored. In this work, we present a systematic MBTI analysis of open-source LLMs across multiple precisions, including mainstream 4-bit methods (GPTQ, AWQ) and extreme 2-bit settings (AQLM variants). Beyond output-level evaluation, we examine how personality emerges across layers through option-level entropy and confidence-gap dynamics, and introduce Uncertainty-Amplified Layer Decoding (UALD) to study decoding-induced personality drift at inference time. Our results reveal a key insight: LLMs' personality is not a static property, but an emergent, layer-dependent decision process sensitive to quantization, prompting, and decoding. Specifically, we find that (1) ENFJ remains dominant across model families and precisions; (2) 4-bit quantization largely preserves coarse personality structure, while 2-bit quantization disrupts fine-grained prompt consistency and cross-precision agreement; (3) personality decisions emerges in upper layers, following substantial ambiguity in early layers; and (4) inference decoding can shift personality, while personality-aligned conditioning improves robustness. These findings provide a new perspective on the behavioral reliability of quantized LLMs and highlight the importance of considering internal dynamics and inference strategies in personality-sensitive chatbot applications.

</details>


### [155] [Distinct dynamics of conceptual and referential disruptions in human reading and large language model processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25999)
*Rui He,Nihal Altay,Wolfram Hinzen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过人为破坏叙事文本中的概念或指代信息，比较人类自定速度阅读与大型语言模型的处理机制。结果表明：概念性破坏引发即时但短暂的处理代价（局部集中），指代性破坏产生较弱但更持久的影响（跨句分布）。语言模型的预测困惑度模式与人类高度相似，而输出层表征显示指代破坏初始扰动更大且遵循幂律衰减。证实概念处理侧重局部整合，指代处理依赖分布式话语身份维护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言意义的两大维度（概念内容与实体指代）在认知加工中的动态差异，尤其关注概念整合与指代消解的处理机制如何区分

Method: 1. 人工构建短叙事，选择性破坏概念信息（如语义异常）或指代信息（如代词错配）  
2. 人类实验：记录自定速度阅读时各词处理时间  
3. 语言模型测试：测量上下文预测困惑度及输出层向量表征位移  
4. 对比分析两类破坏在时间动态和空间分布上的差异模式

Result: • 人类阅读：  
  - 概念破坏：即时出现强处理代价，峰值早且快速衰减（局部集中效应）  
  - 指代破坏：影响较弱，跨词缓慢衰减，受句界调节显著  
• 语言模型：  
  - 预测困惑度：完全复现人类模式（概念效应大而短暂/指代效应小而持久）  
  - 输出表征：指代破坏初始位移更大，两类破坏后续均呈幂律衰减

Conclusion: 首次实证证明概念与指代加工具有本质不同的神经计算特性：概念信息需局部即时整合（高能耗短周期），指代信息依赖全局话语网络（低能耗长周期）。语言模型在表征层面忠实再现人类认知动态，为基于模型的认知假说提供强证据，同时揭示当前模型输出层对指代关系的敏感性超越概念整合。

Abstract: Linguistic meaning is grounded in conceptual content, from which reference to particular entities emerges as words enter discourse. To examine the processing dynamics associated with these two dimensions of meaning, we selectively disrupted conceptual or referential information in short narratives and traced the resulting effects in human self-paced reading and in the predictive and representational processing of large language models. In human reading, conceptual disruptions produced a strong but localized processing cost, emerging immediately after the distorted word, reaching an early maximum, and then declining rapidly. Referential disruptions produced weaker effects, which decreased more gradually across subsequent words, and were more strongly modulated by sentence boundaries. In the language model, both disruptions emerged immediately at the manipulated word. Contextual model surprisal showed a pattern closely paralleling human reading: conceptual disruption produced a larger, more locally concentrated effect that decayed rapidly, whereas referential disruption produced a smaller and more gradual downstream effect. Output-layer representations showed a different pattern: referential disruption produced a larger initial displacement, while both distortions were subsequently characterized by power-law decay. Together, these results provide convergent evidence for distinguishable processing dynamics of two types of meaning: conceptual information imposes a more locally concentrated integration cost, whereas referential information engages a more distributed process of maintaining discourse-level identity.

</details>


### [156] [VISA: Agentic Self-Evolving Data Synthesis for Multimodal Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26013)
*Min Zeng,Guanxin Tan,Libin Cen,Yawei Wen,Rui Hu,Liuyang Bian,Xiaolong Chen,Xiaoxin Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VISA是一个代理框架，将多模态指令合成重构为自我演进循环，通过持续利用失败样本反馈、验证器信号和目标模型错误信息，生成高质量、多样化且具挑战性的训练数据，在MM-IFEval上超越基线同时保持通用多模态能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态指令遵循模型需要准确、多样、可验证且具有挑战性的训练数据。现有合成管道通常采用单轮生成-过滤范式，丢弃了失败样本反馈、验证器输出和目标模型错误等宝贵信息。

Method: VISA通过自我演进循环实现：1）分析图像过滤不兼容约束并发现新的可验证约束；2）从持久内存中采样多样性和难度感知的约束集；3）生成候选指令；4）使用可执行工具和结构化LLM评判者验证样本；5）对失败样本进行诊断引导恢复；6）对接受样本探测目标模型以估计难度；7）将验证器信号和失败档案写回内存，实现约束空间自适应扩展、减少模板重复并聚焦未解决模型弱点；8）验证器合约直接提供强化学习奖励信号，无需单独训练奖励模型。

Result: MM-IFEval实验显示，VISA在强基线方法之上持续提升多模态指令遵循性能，同时在七个公开基准上保持通用多模态能力。

Conclusion: VISA的反馈驱动、自我演进方法有效生成具有挑战性和多样化的多模态指令，超越现有方法同时维持广泛模型能力，为多模态指令合成提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Multimodal instruction-following models require training data that is accurate, diverse, verifiable, and challenging. Existing synthesis pipelines typically follow a one-pass generate-and-filter paradigm, discarding feedback from failed samples, verifier outcomes, and target-model errors. We present VISA (Visual Instruction Synthesis Agent), an agentic framework that reformulates multimodal instruction synthesis as a self-evolving loop. At each round, VISA analyzes an image to filter incompatible constraints and discover new verifiable ones, samples diversity- and difficulty-aware constraint sets from persistent memory, generates candidate instructions, and verifies the resulting samples with executable tools and structured large language model judges. Failed samples trigger diagnostic-guided recovery, while accepted samples are probed against the target model to estimate difficulty. The resulting verifier signals and target-model failure profiles are written back to memory, allowing subsequent rounds to adaptively expand the constraint space, reduce template repetition, and focus on unresolved model weaknesses. The same verifier contracts further provide reward signals for reinforcement learning without a separately trained reward model. Experiments on MM-IFEval show that VISA consistently improves multimodal instruction following over strong baselines, while preserving general multimodal capability across seven public benchmarks.

</details>


### [157] [Beyond Local Surprise: Grounded Dialogue as Selective Belief Revision under Referential Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26035)
*Ziming Liu,Bhanu Chaitanya Jasti,Ziyang Xu,Hongyu Wu,Yi Wu,Jiqun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究对话中听众在面对不可见场景描述时，如何在保持当前理解与修正理解之间做出决策。研究发现，仅基于局部错配进行更新的策略会导致对话基础不稳定，而基于累积不确定性的策略能更好地平衡连贯性与检索性能。有趣的是，局部错配反而促进保持理解，而累积的不确定性才促进修正，这一模式与概念协议理论一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当说话者描述听众无法直接看到的场景时，听众必须决定是保持当前理解还是根据新话语进行修正。现有语言系统通常将局部错配作为更新的信号，但对话理解可能更为保守，倾向于相对于先前理解来解释不匹配的证据，而非立即修正。

Method: 研究者引入了一种受控的、数据驱动的话语间保存/修正决策框架，在相同条件下学习竞争性修正策略。比较了四种理论驱动的修正策略，每种策略反映了关于听众何时应保持或修正理解的不同假设。

Result: 主要发现：1）基于错配的策略仅根据局部分歧进行更新，对不匹配反应强烈，但破坏了对话基础并降低了检索性能；2）敏感性策略在错配更新基础上结合了累积证据，在保持连贯理解的同时维持了强大的检索性能。出乎意料的是，连贯理解的产生源于一种反直觉的模式：局部错配促进保持，而累积的不确定性促进修正。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了对话理解中一个反直觉的修正机制：听众在面临局部不匹配时倾向于保持先前理解，只有当不确定性充分累积时才进行修正。这一发现与概念协议理论相一致，表明对话理解是相对保守的，需要充分的证据积累才会调整理解。

Abstract: When a speaker refers to a scene that the listener cannot directly see, the listener must decide whether to preserve its current understanding or revise it as new utterances arrive. Many language systems treat local mismatch as a cue for updating: divergence from the current understanding encourages adjustment. Yet conversational understanding may be more conservative, interpreting mismatching evidence relative to prior understanding rather than immediately revising it. We introduce a controlled, data-driven framework for turn-by-turn preserve/revise decisions in dialogue, where competing revision policies are learned under otherwise identical conditions. We compare four theory-driven revision strategies, each reflecting a different assumption about when listeners should preserve or revise. Two findings stand out. First, a mismatch-driven policy that updates solely based on local divergence reacts strongly to mismatch but destabilizes grounding and degrades retrieval. Second, an uncertainty-sensitive policy extends mismatch-based updating with accumulated evidence, preserving coherent understanding while maintaining strong retrieval performance. Surprisingly, coherent understanding emerges from a counterintuitive pattern: local mismatch promotes preservation, whereas accumulated uncertainty promotes revision, suggesting that listeners maintain prior understanding despite local mismatch and revise only when uncertainty sufficiently accumulates. This pattern is consistent with conceptual pact theory.

</details>


### [158] [Fine-Tuning Whisper for Automatic Speech Recognition in Baniwa: A Preliminary Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26060)
*Leonardo Duart,Tiago Fonseca,Thiago Chacón*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Whisper模型被成功微调用于Baniwa语（一种资源极度匮乏的南美原住民语言）的自动语音识别任务，取得了7.45%的字错误率，为未来研究奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管ASR技术在近年取得了显著进展，但大多数进展集中在高资源语言上，而原住民语言仍然面临语音资源和语言技术缺乏的问题。

Method: 研究者使用来自语言学文献记录项目的1,373条手动转录录音（约0.54小时语音，主要为孤立单词和短促诱发语句），对Whisper Small模型进行监督学习微调，并使用词错误率(WER)和字错误率(CER)进行评估。

Result: 最佳模型实现了37.5%的WER和7.45%的CER，证明了多语言基础模型可以成功适配到资源极度匮乏的原住民语言中。

Conclusion: 研究结果为Baniwa语ASR建立了初始基线，并为未来涉及更大数据集、语言特定适配策略和后处理技术的研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) technologies have achieved remarkable performance in recent years through the use of large multilingual foundation models. However, most advances remain concentrated on high-resource languages, while indigenous languages continue to suffer from a lack of speech resources and language technologies. This work presents a preliminary study on the adaptation of Whisper for Automatic Speech Recognition in Baniwa, an indigenous Arawakan language spoken in Brazil, Colombia, and Venezuela. The experiments were conducted using a corpus of 1,373 manually transcribed recordings obtained from a linguistic documentation project. The corpus contains approximately 0.54 hours of speech and consists primarily of isolated words and short elicited utterances. The Whisper Small model was fine-tuned using supervised learning and evaluated using Word Error Rate (WER) and Character Error Rate (CER). The best model achieved a WER of 37.5% and a CER of 7.45%, demonstrating that multilingual foundation models can be successfully adapted to extremely low-resource indigenous languages. The results establish an initial baseline for Baniwa Automatic Speech Recognition and provide a foundation for future research involving larger datasets, language-specific adaptation strategies, and post-processing techniques.

</details>


### [159] [Prefix Sliding for efficient test-time scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26070)
*Niklas Muennighoff,Zhengyang Wang,Zeyi Chen,Weijia Shi,Binyuan Hui,John Yang,Dapeng Jiang,Mika Senghaas,Fares Obeid,Johannes Hagemann,Sami Jaghouar,Ludwig Schmidt,Percy Liang,Jason Wei,Andrew Y. Ng,Luke Zettlemoyer,Yejin Choi,Mike Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Prefix Sliding 通过仅在推理过程中保留前缀（关键指令和工具）和最近数千个 token 的窗口，丢弃其他中间推理 token，从而在不降低性能的前提下实现 3 倍推理速度提升，并通过 RL 训练可扩展到 10 万+ token 的推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时缩放（test-time scaling）通过增加测试时计算量提升模型性能，但硬性任务需要长时间推理，模型通过完整注意力机制保留整个推理轨迹在内存中，导致成本过高。研究发现大部分中间推理 token 随着推理继续会逐渐失去重要性，质疑保留这些 token 是否值得。

Method: 提出 Prefix Sliding 方法，在推理过程中丢弃不属于前缀或最近数千 token 窗口内的 token。前缀包含模型可用的关键指令和工具，最近 token 窗口则是模型当前正在处理的推理内容。该方法无论推理时长如何，都能限制总内存需求，实现高效的长周期测试时缩放。

Result: 无训练情况下，Prefix Sliding 可使现有模型推理速度提升 3 倍且保持性能；通过 RL 训练使用 Prefix Sliding 可获得更好的性能，使模型能扩展到超过十万 token 的推理轨迹；消融实验表明 Prefix Sliding 优于中间 token 摘要或普通滑动窗口方法。

Conclusion: Prefix Sliding 通过智能的 token 丢弃策略，在不损失性能的前提下大幅降低内存需求和推理时间，实现了高效的长周期测试时缩放，为语言模型的推理增强提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Test-time scaling uses extra test-time compute to improve performance, such as letting language models reason longer when solving a problem. As models keep the entire reasoning trace in memory via full attention, hard tasks that need long thinking can be prohibitively expensive. However, we find most intermediate reasoning tokens lose importance as the model continues reasoning. This calls into question whether retaining them is worth the cost. Based on this insight, we propose Prefix Sliding, which discards tokens during reasoning that are not part of the prefix or the window of the last few thousand tokens. The prefix has key instructions and tools available to the model, while the most recent tokens are the current reasoning the model is working on. This caps the total memory requirement regardless of how long the model reasons, allowing for efficient long-horizon test-time scaling. Without training, Prefix Sliding can make existing models 3x faster while maintaining performance. Training with Prefix Sliding using reinforcement learning can achieve better performance by enabling scaling to reasoning traces beyond a hundred thousand tokens. Ablations show Prefix Sliding outperforms summarizing intermediate tokens or vanilla sliding window. Our code is at https://github.com/Muennighoff/prefix-sliding

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Security-Aware Pinching-Antenna Systems (PASS): Physical-Layer Security Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25301)
*Zhaoming Hu,Xiaochen Nie,Ruikang Zhong,Haochen Li,Dengao Li,Xidong Mu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对捏压天线系统（PASS）中的异构安全多用户传输问题，提出统一的角色依赖框架，包含低、中、高三种安全模式，并通过两种基于学习的控制器（HSPPO和MRHA-DPO）实现信息波束成形、人工噪声和天线位置的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理层安全设计仅通过权重或阈值表示不同的安全需求，而异构服务可能为每个信息流改变每个接收器的逻辑角色。需要建立能够灵活支持服务相关保密要求并平衡在线效率与控制能力的统一框架。

Method: 建立统一的基于角色的PASS传输框架，包含三种安全模式：低安全模式最大化合法用户最小速率，中安全模式保护机密流免受外部窃听者攻击，高安全模式防止非目标合法用户拦截未授权信息。将信息波束成形、人工噪声和天线位置的联合优化公式化为长时域连续控制问题，并开发两种基于学习的控制器：异构安全感知近端策略优化（HSPPO）和多重关系层次感知扩散策略优化（MRHA-DPO）。

Result: 提出的框架使通用PASS平台能够灵活支持服务相关的保密要求，同时平衡在线效率和控制能力。HSPPO通过直接将模式特定的速率和保密性违规转化为归一化平滑反馈嵌入优势函数中，实现轻量级和违规敏感控制；MRHA-DPO结合PASS感知多关系图编码器、图条件层次速度网络和精确反演DPO训练，实现拓扑感知和表达性控制。

Conclusion: 该方法为捏压天线系统中的异构安全多用户传输提供了互补的复杂性-性能权衡，通过基于角色的安全模式和两种学习控制器的组合，实现了灵活、高效的安全传输方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates heterogeneous secure multi-user transmission in pinching-antenna systems (PASS), where dynamically adjustable pinching antennas reshape both guided-wave and free-space propagation to improve communication and confidentiality performance. Unlike conventional physical-layer security designs that represent different security requirements merely through weights or thresholds, heterogeneous services may change the logical role of each receiver for each information stream. To address this issue, we establish a unified role-dependent PASS transmission framework comprising low-, medium-, and high-security modes. These modes respectively maximize the minimum legitimate-user rate, protect confidential streams against external eavesdroppers, and further prevent non-target legitimate users from intercepting unauthorized information. The resulting joint optimization of information beamforming, artificial noise, and pinching-antenna positions is formulated as a long-horizon continuous-control problem. Two learning-based controllers are then developed to provide complementary complexity-performance tradeoffs. First, heterogeneous security-aware proximal policy optimization (HSPPO) directly transforms mode-specific rate and secrecy violations into normalized smooth feedback embedded in the proximal-policy-optimization advantage, enabling lightweight and violation-sensitive control. Second, multi-relational hierarchy-aware diffusion policy optimization (MRHA-DPO) combines a PASS-aware multi-relational graph encoder, a graph-conditioned hierarchical velocity network, and exact-inversion DPO training to achieve topology-aware and expressive control. The proposed framework enables a common PASS platform to flexibly support service-dependent confidentiality requirements while balancing online efficiency and control capability.

</details>


### [161] [End-to-End Mobility-Aware Multi-RIS Optimization via Blockage Detection and Closed-Form Riemannian Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25393)
*Sehyun Ryu,Seungmin Choi,Hyun Jong Yang,John M. Cioffi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的移动感知多RIS优化框架，通过RIS阻塞检测和Riemannian优化提升毫米波MIMO系统在动态阻塞环境下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波多用户MIMO系统易受动态阻塞影响，RIS被引入作为解决方案，但RIS链路本身也可能被阻塞，而现有研究通常假设理想可用性。

Method: 提出端到端的移动感知多RIS优化框架，集成每个RIS的阻塞检测和闭式Riemannian更新。基站传输短索引同步信号，用户通过简单能量测试识别被阻塞的面板。基于检测到的可行集，使用随机闭式Riemannian相位对齐（SCRPA）算法联合优化基站预编码器和RIS相位，确保单位模约束可行、单调收敛和低复杂度。

Result: 大量仿真验证了可靠的阻塞检测能力，与现有基准相比，实现了显著的加权吞吐量和可扩展性提升。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了毫米波系统中RIS链路的动态阻塞问题，通过联合优化策略显著提升了系统性能和可扩展性。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) multi-user MIMO systems are highly susceptible to dynamic blockages, and reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) have been introduced as a remedy. However, RIS links can themselves be blocked, while existing studies often assume ideal availability. This paper proposes an end-to-end mobility-aware multi-RIS optimization framework that integrates per-RIS blockage detection with closed-form Riemannian updates. The base station transmits short indexed synchronization signals, enabling each user to identify blocked panels via a simple energy test. Based on the detected feasible sets, we jointly optimize the BS precoder and RIS phases using a Stochastic Closed-form Riemannian Phase Alignment (SCRPA) algorithm, which ensures unit-modulus feasibility, monotone convergence, and low complexity. Extensive simulations validate reliable blockage detection and demonstrate significant weighted sum-rate and scalability gains compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [162] [Token-Oriented Semantic Communication with Pretrained Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25410)
*Jiwoong Im,Minwoo Kim,Jaeho Lee,Yo-Seb Jeon,Yongjune Kim*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向令牌（token）的语义通信框架，通过令牌级别的任务相关性选择性地传输压缩图像潜变量，实现了令牌粒度的通信而无需直接传输令牌嵌入。该框架模块化地协调三个预训练组件：轻量客户端视觉Transformer、学习型图像压缩模型和大型服务器端视觉Transformer，无需端到端训练。实验表明，该框架在ImageNet上实现了优于现有语义通信方案、手工编码器和任务无关学习型压缩模型的速率-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 令牌通信在Transformer令牌粒度上实现语义通信原理，为资源受限的边缘系统中的客户端-服务器协同推理提供了有前景的方向。然而，直接传输令牌嵌入面临两大挑战：高昂的通信成本以及跨模型特定令牌嵌入空间的互操作性有限。

Method: 提出一种面向令牌的语义通信框架。框架中，令牌级别的任务相关性决定传输哪些压缩图像潜变量，从而实现令牌粒度传输而无需直接传输令牌嵌入。框架模块化地协调三个预训练组件：轻量客户端视觉Transformer（ViT）、学习型图像压缩（LIC）模型和大型服务器端ViT，无需端到端训练。关键使能技术是ViT补丁令牌与LIC潜变量之间的一对一空间对齐，使得令牌级别的任务相关性可以直接决定传输哪些潜向量。基于此对齐，令牌对齐的LIC选择性传输任务相关潜变量；层选择性注意力展开在单次前向传递中从选定的注意力层范围估计令牌相关性；代理令牌替换通过优化单个可学习令牌来适应冻结的服务器模型。

Result: 在ImageNet上的实验表明，所提出的框架实现了比近期语义通信方案、手工编码器和任务无关LIC模型更优的速率-精度权衡。

Conclusion: 本文提出的面向令牌的语义通信框架通过仅传输任务相关的压缩潜变量，有效降低了通信成本并提高了互操作性，实现了更优的速率-精度权衡。

Abstract: Token communications realize the semantic communication principle at the granularity of transformer tokens, providing a promising direction for client--server collaborative inference in resource-constrained edge systems. However, directly transmitting token embeddings presents two practical challenges: substantial communication cost and limited interoperability across model-specific token embedding spaces. To address these challenges, we propose a \emph{token-oriented} semantic communication framework. In this framework, token-level task relevance determines which compressed image latents are transmitted, enabling token-granular transmission without directly transmitting token embeddings. The framework is modular, coordinating three pretrained components---a lightweight client-side vision transformer (ViT), a learned image compression (LIC) model, and a large server-side ViT---without end-to-end training. The key enabler is the one-to-one spatial alignment between ViT patch tokens and the LIC latent vectors, which allows token-level task relevance to directly determine which latent vectors are transmitted. Building on this alignment, token-aligned LIC selectively transmits task-relevant latents, layer-selective attention rollout estimates token relevance from a selected range of attention layers in a single forward pass, and surrogate token substitution adapts the frozen server model by optimizing a single learnable token. Experiments on ImageNet show that the proposed framework achieves a more favorable rate--accuracy trade-off than recent semantic communication schemes, hand-crafted codecs, and task-agnostic LIC models.

</details>


### [163] [BeamGuard: Risk-Aware Multimodal Beam Forecasting and Adaptive Virtual Beamwidth Control for 6G mmWave V2I Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25433)
*Abidemi Orimogunje,Dejan Vukobratovic,Sunwoo Kim,Igbafe Orikumhi,Vukan Ninkovic,Evariste Twahirwa,Gaspard Gashema*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: BeamGuard是一种多模态感知辅助的6G毫米波V2I波束管理框架，通过融合外部传感器数据和可选的部分带内毫米波功率观测来预测未来波束分布，并利用风险感知规划器选择自适应虚拟波束宽度动作，在多种场景下实现了可靠的V2I控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G毫米波车辆到基础设施(V2I)链路中，窄波束虽能提供高阵列增益，但易受移动引起的失配、遮挡和域变化影响，可靠的波束管理成为核心挑战。

Method: BeamGuard框架融合摄像头、雷达、激光雷达、GPS和毫米波功率观测，通过时域多模态预测器预测后验分布，再由风险感知规划器将预测结果转换为波束中心和虚拟码本级波束宽度。支持纯传感器操作（减少波束训练开销）、有限带内操作（掩码波束功率条目）和全混合操作（感知与通信侧测量）。

Result: 在DeepSense 6G场景32和33上评估，并在场景31和34上进行留出测试。全混合锚点（完整系统参考）的Top-1/Top-3/Top-5准确率约为0.393/0.778/0.897，规划器基于阈值的 outage概率约为0.0060，增益比约为0.895。与匹配预算的基线比较显示，BeamGuard在可比带内观测设置下优于多层感知机、循环和时域卷积预测器。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，通过多模态预测和风险感知虚拟波束宽度控制，BeamGuard实现了鲁棒的、开销感知的波束管理，为6G毫米波V2I链路提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable beam management is a central challenge for 6G millimeter-wave (mmWave) vehicle-to-infrastructure (V2I) links, where narrow beams provide high array gain but are vulnerable to mobility-induced misalignment, blockage, and domain variation. BeamGuard is a multimodal sensing-aided beam-management framework that combines exteroceptive sensing with optional partial in-band mmWave power observations to forecast future beam distributions and select adaptive virtual beamwidth actions for reliable V2I control. It fuses camera, radar, LiDAR, GPS, and mmWave power observations with a temporal multimodal forecaster, then converts the predicted posterior into a beam center and virtual codebook-level beamwidth through a risk-aware planner. Here, virtual beamwidth denotes adjacent-beam coverage in the codebook index space rather than physical analog wide-beam synthesis. BeamGuard supports sensor-only operation for beam-training overhead reduction, limited in-band operation with masked beam-power entries, and full hybrid operation with sensing and communication-side measurements. We evaluate BeamGuard on DeepSense 6G Scenarios 32 and 33, with additional held-out tests on Scenarios 31 and 34, covering day--night training, transfer, limited adaptation, ablations, budget sweeps, and lightweight baselines. The full-hybrid anchor, used as the complete-system reference, achieves Top-1/Top-3/Top-5 accuracies of approximately \(0.393/0.778/0.897\), while the planner attains a threshold-based outage probability of about \(0.0060\) with a gain ratio of about \(0.895\). Matched-budget baselines further show that BeamGuard improves over multilayer perceptron, recurrent, and temporal convolutional predictors under comparable in-band observation settings. These results demonstrate robust, overhead-aware beam management through multimodal forecasting and risk-aware virtual beamwidth control.

</details>


### [164] [Near-Field Dual-UPA Communications: A Generalized Geometric Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25510)
*Li Zheng,Xing Hao,Ziru Chen,Yong Liu,Li Chen,Lin X. Cai*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究配备双均匀平面阵列的近场MIMO系统，提出通用几何模型计算收发阵元间三维距离，推导近场-远场边界，将信道矩阵分解为Kronecker积以实现低复杂度波束成形，数值结果验证分析并表明传统瑞利距离是该模型的特例，所提波束成形方法在接近最优速率的同时显著降低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近场MIMO通信利用大尺寸天线阵列在近场区域实现更精确的波束成形，但现有研究缺乏对双UPA配置的通用几何建模和低复杂度波束成形设计。

Method: 建立收发面板间任意天线阵元的三维距离通用几何模型，推导双UPA配置下近场-远场边界的闭式表达式，利用UPA的几何结构将近场信道矩阵分解为两个低维矩阵的Kronecker积，基于此分解设计低复杂度近场波束成形方案以最大化可实现速率。

Result: 数值结果验证了理论分析，证明传统瑞利距离是通用模型的特殊情形，所提波束成形方案在达到接近最优速率的同时，计算复杂度显著低于现有先进近场波束成形方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的通用几何模型和信道矩阵分解方法为双UPA近场MIMO系统提供了新的理论框架和高效波束成形设计，克服了传统远场假设的局限性，为未来近场通信系统实现奠定了技术基础。

Abstract: This paper investigates a near-field (NF) multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) communication system equipped with dual uniform planar arrays (UPAs). We first develop a generalized geometric model to calculate the 3D distance between arbitrary antenna elements across the transmitter and receiver panels. Leveraging the distance analysis, we derive a closed-form near-field to far-field (NF-FF) boundary for dual-UPA configurations. By exploiting the geometric structure of the UPAs, we further decompose the near-field channel matrix into a Kronecker-product of two lower-dimensional matrices. This decomposition enables a low-complexity NF beamforming design for achievable-rate maximization. Numerical results validate the analysis and demonstrate that the conventional Rayleigh distance is a special case of the generalized model. Furthermore, the proposed beamforming design achieves near-optimal rate performance while significantly reducing the computational complexity compared to state-of-the-art NF beamforming methods.

</details>


### [165] [Power from Space: Coordinated Satellite Charging for Off-Grid Wireless Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25589)
*Osmel M. Rosabal,Amirhossein Azarbahram,Mateen Ashraf,Mohammad Shehab,Abdul Basit Khattak,Onel L. A. López,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文系统性地探讨了卫星无线能量传输技术，包括微波远距离充电的优缺点、多卫星协同传输的愿景、两种协调方案的接收功率评估、激光星际能量传输性能分析、超表面集成优化方案，以及面临的挑战和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统能源技术难以高效满足离网场景下物联网设备的紧急能源需求，卫星无线能量传输作为变革性解决方案具有巨大潜力，但需要系统性研究其技术实现路径和优化方案。

Method: 1. 综述微波远距离充电的优势与局限性；2. 提出多卫星协同的空间WPT愿景并分析应用场景；3. 采用两种协调方案评估目标位置的接收功率；4. 统计特征化非目标区域的功率溢出；5. 评估不同距离和孔径下星际激光WPT性能；6. 探索超表面在卫星孔径和地面网络中的集成应用。

Result: 1. 明确了微波WPT的技术边界；2. 提出了多卫星协同服务地面网络的可行框架；3. 量化了两种协调方案的功率传输效率差异；4. 建立了功率溢出的统计模型；5. 确定了星际激光WPT的最优工作距离和孔径配置；6. 验证了超表面技术对能量转换效率、可扩展性和波束管理的提升效果。

Conclusion: 卫星无线能量传输具备实现全球物联网设备无线充电的技术潜力，多卫星协同配合超表面优化是可行的技术路径，但仍面临能量效率、安全性和标准化等关键挑战，需要进一步研究突破。

Abstract: Satellite-enabled wireless power transfer (WPT) may be a transformative solution for charging Internet of Things (IoT) devices in off-grid scenarios where traditional technologies struggle to efficiently meet urgent energy demands. In this article, we review the advantages and limitations of microwave-based long-distance charging for satellite-enabled WPT. We then introduce our vision of coordinated space-based WPT, where multiple satellites jointly serve networks of ground devices. Potential use cases are presented highlighting application requirements. We evaluate the average received power at the target locations using two coordination schemes and perform a statistical characterization of the power spillover on undesired locations. We also shed light on the performance of inter-satellite laser WPT for different operating distances and transmit-receive apertures of the peer satellites. Moreover, we explore the integration of metasurfaces on satellite apertures and ground networks to boost energy conversion efficiency, scalability, and beam management. Finally, we outline relevant challenges and research directions towards implementing our vision.

</details>


### [166] [Multi-UE Networked Sensing: A New Paradigm for 6G Perceptive Mobile Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25597)
*J. Andrew Zhang,Jingying Bao,Kai Wu,Henk Wymeersch,Christos Masouros,Y. Jay Guo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出多用户设备(UE)感知这一新型网络化感知范式，通过利用分布式用户设备与共同目标相互作用产生的相关感知观测，释放集成感知与通信(ISAC)系统的全部感知潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化感知通过联合利用多个分布式节点的观测信息，对于充分发挥集成感知与通信系统的全部感知潜力至关重要。现有感知技术尚未充分利用分布式用户设备产生的相关感知观测。

Method: 提出多UE感知架构，包括上行、下行和混合感知架构的代表性方案，并建立涵盖同步、相关性感知参数估计和感知融合的多视角信号处理框架。

Result: 系统展示了多UE感知的理论框架和架构设计，识别并讨论了四个关键挑战：相关性建模、目标关联、感知信息压缩和通信-感知联合优化。

Conclusion: 多UE感知为未来感知型移动网络提供了新的网络化感知范式，通过利用分布式设备间的自然相关性，可显著提升感知能力，但相关性建模、目标关联等关键技术挑战仍需深入研究。

Abstract: Networked sensing, which jointly exploits observations from multiple distributed nodes, is essential for unlocking the full sensing potential of integrated sensing and communications (ISAC). This article introduces multi-UE sensing, a new networked sensing paradigm for future perceptive mobile networks that exploits the correlated sensing observations naturally arising from distributed user equipment devices (UEs) interacting with common targets. Representative uplink, downlink, and hybrid sensing architectures are presented, together with a multi-view signal processing framework encompassing synchronization, correlation-aware parameter estimation, and sensing fusion. Key open challenges, including correlation modelling, target association, sensing information compression, and communication-sensing co-optimization, are also discussed.

</details>


### [167] [A Subcarrier-Aware Approach for Robust Respiratory Monitoring with Commodity Wi-Fi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25612)
*Pei Tang,Yunpeng Ge,Ivan Wang-Hei Ho*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种子载波感知Wi-Fi感知框架，通过表征子载波依赖性呼吸敏感性实现接触式呼吸监测，达到了97%的呼吸率估计准确率，相比基线方法将平均绝对误差降低了0.45次/分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wi-Fi感知因无处不在的特性在家庭医疗保健中成为接触式呼吸监测的有前景模态，但传统方法通常将信道状态信息（CSI）子载波统一处理，忽视了它们对呼吸运动的不同响应，限制了监测性能。

Method: 提出了子载波感知感知框架：1）表征子载波依赖性呼吸敏感性并进行信息性子载波选择；2）基于呼吸敏感子载波的统计特性开发无监督聚类呼吸估计方法；3）针对呼吸暂停检测提出幅度衰减阈值机制。

Result: 实验结果显示：1）呼吸率估计准确率达到97%；2）相比基线方法MAE降低0.45次/分钟；3）呼吸暂停情况下MAE为1.6次/分钟；4）在不同场景下均展现出稳健性和有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架在不同场景下均表现出稳健性和有效性，展示了在接触式家庭医疗保健监测中的潜力，为无接触呼吸监测提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Wi-Fi sensing has emerged as a promising modality for contact-free respiratory monitoring in home healthcare due to its ubiquity. However, conventional approaches typically treat Channel State Information (CSI) subcarriers uniformly, neglecting their heterogeneous responses to breathing motions. To address this limitation, we propose a subcarrier-aware sensing framework that characterizes subcarrier-dependent respiratory sensitivities and exploits them for informative subcarrier selection. Based on the statistical properties of breathing-sensitive subcarriers, we further develop an unsupervised clustering-based breathing estimation method for robust respiratory monitoring. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves 97% breathing rate estimation accuracy and reduces the mean absolute error (MAE) by 0.45 breaths per minute (bpm) compared with baseline methods. Furthermore, we extend the analysis to breathing-pause detection, which derives an amplitude-attenuation-based threshold mechanism. Under breathing pauses, the proposed method achieves an MAE of 1.6 bpm in breathing rate estimation. These results demonstrate the robustness and effectiveness of the proposed framework across different scenarios and its potential for contactless home healthcare monitoring.

</details>


### [168] [Statistical Analysis of Primary and Random Clusters in 318 GHz Terahertz Channels for Industrial IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25634)
*Siyuan Shao,Peize Zhang,Pekka Kyösti,Trung Q. Duong,Simon L. Cotton*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文在工业环境中对318 GHz太赫兹传播信道进行了统计分析，提出了针对稀疏多径分量的新聚类方案，发现大尺度参数主要受强反射影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太赫兹通信提供的超高数据速率满足工业物联网应用需求，使得工业环境中太赫兹信道的研究成为关键课题。

Method: 在工业环境中对318 GHz传播信道进行综合统计分析，提出针对多径分量稀疏性的新聚类方案，分别对主集群（强反射）和随机集群进行统计分析。

Result: 大尺度参数主要受强反射（主集群）的影响。

Conclusion: 本研究为后续太赫兹随机信道建模提供了可靠的支撑和指导。

Abstract: The ultra-high data rates enabled by terahertz (THz) communications pave the way for the demanding requirements of industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) applications, making the investigation of THz channels in industrial environments a critical research topic. This paper presents a comprehensive statistical analysis of the propagation channel at 318\,GHz in an industrial environment. In particular, a new clustering scheme is proposed for the sparsity observed in the multipath components (MPCs) of the measured channel. Furthermore, statistical analyses are conducted separately for the group of strong reflections, defined as primary clusters, and other propagation phenomena, defined as random clusters, in a rich-scattering environment. The results demonstrate that the large-scale parameters are predominantly influenced by these strong reflections. This study provides reliable support and guidance for subsequent THz stochastic channel modeling.

</details>


### [169] [Semi-Blind Channel Estimation for Dynamic NTN Systems via Spiked Random Matrix Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25694)
*Xue Zhang,Abla Kammoun,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向非地面网络(NTN)多用户上行系统的鲁棒半盲信道估计框架。该方法通过随机矩阵理论优化正则化参数，在高维动态环境中显著提升了信道估计精度，且计算效率高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NTN等高度动态环境中，快速变化的信道和高系统维度导致传统基于协方差的估计器受采样噪声影响严重，难以在导频开销和估计精度之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出了一种基于最优正则化最小二乘公式的鲁棒半盲估计方法。该方法结合训练信息导引和盲子空间结构，利用随机矩阵理论(RMT)中的尖峰协方差模型推导信道均方误差(MSE)的闭式表达式，并解析地求解最优正则化参数。

Result: 推导出了信道MSE的闭式表征和最优正则化参数的解析设计。该估计器计算高效，尤其适合高维场景。在3GPP NTN信道模型下的仿真显示，该方法在性能上优于传统的半盲和基于训练的估计器。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效解决了NTN环境下的信道估计难题，在降低导频开销的同时保证了估计精度，是处理大规模无线系统动态信道挑战的有效方案。

Abstract: Semi-blind channel estimation offers an attractive tradeoff between pilot overhead and estimation accuracy in large-scale wireless systems. However, reliable channel acquisition becomes particularly challenging in highly dynamic environments such as non-terrestrial networks (NTNs), where rapidly varying channels and high system dimensionality significantly degrade the performance of conventional covariance-based estimators due to sampling noise. In this paper, we propose a robust semi-blind channel estimation framework for multi-user uplink systems operating in NTN systems. The proposed approach introduces an optimally regularized least-squares formulation that balances training-based information and blind subspace structure. By exploiting the spiked covariance model within a random matrix theory (RMT) framework, we derive a closed-form characterization of the resulting channel mean-squared error and obtain an analytically tractable design of the optimal regularization parameter. The resulting estimator is computationally efficient and particularly well suited to high-dimensional regimes. Simulation results under realistic Third Generation Partnership Project (3GPP) NTN channel models demonstrate substantial performance improvements over conventional semi-blind and training-based estimators.

</details>


### [170] [Output-Only Identification and Spectral Monitoring of Coupled Feedback Networks with Known Time-Varying Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25844)
*Jihwan Woo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文提出在仅有输出且无外部激励的设定下，通过对已知增益时变性和部分反转时刻的结构利用，实现耦合反馈矩阵L_t=Φ diag(γ_t)的识别，并给出严格局部可识别性、一阶交互估计量及其√T渐近性质、交叉识别定理和bootstrap有效性，仿真与杠杆基金再平衡案例验证了方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合反馈网络常被逐通道监测，但跨通道路径会同时影响稳定性裕度和传递扰动。然而实际中往往只能观测到时间分离的输出和调度增益γ_t，耦合响应Φ和清除窗口输入不可观测，缺乏有指令、探测或参考输入。需要在输出仅有的情况下识别结构化反馈矩阵。

Method: 识别依赖于两方面：观测到的预窗口输出的持续激励，以及两个将耦合与混淆分离的结构特征——增益的已知时变性以可预测模式调节闭环响应，以及部分反转时刻（在后续窗口中已知比例的瞬态位移被纠正）。论文给出结果层级：在增益制度满足雅可比秩条件下耦合的精确局部可识别；一阶交互估计量，其可识别强度为残差化交互信息矩阵的最小特征值（在常数增益下可证不可识别）；将估计量表征为resolvent灵敏度，用于筛选传递扰动和作为谱裕度恢复的第一阶段输入；给出√T渐近性、将每个变化增益映射到精确识别的resolvent行列的交叉识别定理，以及在一致选择下的bootstrap有效性；实现启发式的经验覆盖率（名义95%下为90%）量化剩余缺口。

Result: 仿真验证了秩条件的锐利性并量化了混淆下的基准失败。杠杆基金再平衡反馈案例研究中，每日基金披露扮演已知增益的角色，在真实数据上展示了该方法。

Conclusion: 在仅有输出且无外部激励的场景下，利用增益时变性和部分反转时刻的结构特性，可以实现耦合反馈矩阵的精确局部可识别和一阶交互估计，并提供具有√T渐近性质和bootstrap有效性的估计工具。仿真与真实案例表明该方法能有效应对混淆并提供实用的识别方案。

Abstract: Coupled feedback networks are often monitored channel by channel even though cross-channel paths alter both stability margins and transmitted disturbances. We study identification of a structured feedback matrix L_t = Phi diag(gamma_t) in an output-only setting: no commanded, probing, or reference input exists -- only temporally separated outputs and the scheduling gains gamma_t are observed, while the coupling response Phi and the clearing-window inputs are not. Identification rests jointly on the persistent excitation of the observed pre-window output and on two structural features separating coupling from confounds: the known time variation of the gains, which modulates the closed-loop response in a predictable pattern, and a partial-reversal moment by which a known fraction of transient displacement is corrected in a subsequent window. We give a hierarchy of results: exact local identification of the coupling under a Jacobian rank condition on the gain regimes; a first-order interaction estimator whose identification strength is the minimum eigenvalue of the residualized interaction information matrix (provably unidentified under constant gains); and a characterization of the estimand as a resolvent sensitivity -- the right object for screening transmitted disturbances and a first-stage input to spectral-margin recovery -- with sqrt(T) asymptotics for the first-order estimator, a cross-identification theorem mapping each varying gain to exactly identified resolvent rows and columns, and bootstrap validity under consistent selection; the implemented heuristic's empirical coverage (90% at nominal 95%) quantifies the remaining gap. Simulations verify sharpness of the rank condition and quantify benchmark failures under confounding. A case study on leveraged-fund rebalancing feedback, where daily fund disclosures play the role of the known gains, illustrates the method on real data.

</details>


### [171] [Efficient DCT-Based Estimation and Compensation of Nonlinear Channels for OFDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25847)
*Marc Martinez-Gost,Ana Pérez-Neira,Miguel Ángel Lagunas*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最大似然估计的OFDM系统非线性频率选择性信道估计框架，采用DCT表示法建模非线性失真，实现了低复杂度实时实现和接近理想的BER性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OFDM通信系统中非线性失真（幅度和相位非线性）严重影响信道估计精度和系统性能，需要开发能够有效处理非线性失真、计算复杂度低且适合实时实现的高效信道估计算法。

Method: 采用最大似然(ML)框架估计非线性频率选择性信道，利用离散余弦变换(DCT)表示法建模非线性失真，形成条件良好的估计问题，具有良好的收敛特性，结合紧凑参数化实现低计算复杂度和快速自适应能力。

Result: 提出的信道估计方法可与多种非线性补偿方法配合使用，在有限训练开销下实现接近理想的BER性能；在存在幅度和相位非线性时性能保持至约15dB SNR，仅存在幅度失真时保持至-10dB SNR。

Conclusion: 基于DCT表示的最大似然信道估计框架有效解决了OFDM系统非线性信道估计问题，在多种非线性失真条件下实现了优异性能和计算效率，适合实时部署应用。

Abstract: This paper proposes a maximum-likelihood (ML) framework for estimating nonlinear frequency-selective channels in orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) communication systems. The nonlinear distortions are modeled using a Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT)-based representation, which results in a well-conditioned estimation problem with favorable convergence properties. The proposed method combines a compact parameterization with low computational complexity, enabling fast adaptation and efficient real-time implementation. Numerical results show that the proposed channel estimation can be used for multiple nonlinear compensation methods and achieve near-ideal BER performance with very limited training overhead. This performance is maintained down to approximately 15 dB SNR in the presence of both amplitude and phase nonlinearities, and down to -10 dB when only amplitude distortions are present.

</details>


### [172] [Visualizing Wireless Propagation and Polarization in Augmented Reality with ESPARGOS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25996)
*Florian Euchner,Stephan ten Brink*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种增强现实可视化系统，通过将ESPARGOS天线阵列的相位相干WiFi信道测量数据实时叠加到摄像头画面上，使无线多径传播、波束成形和极化等不可见射频现象变得直观可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线射频场对人类不可见，导致无线多径传播、波束成形和极化等核心概念难以直接观察。这一挑战阻碍了科学传播、教学和实验调试工作的开展。

Method: 系统利用ESPARGOS天线阵列采集相位相干的WiFi信道测量数据，估计二维波束空间表示，将其与光学摄像头视图进行配准，并将角度谱叠加到物理场景上。通过Jones计算从两个独立天线馈源的信道测量中计算极化信息，额外显示相对路径延迟和极化信息层。

Result: 实现了一个直观的"WiFi摄像头"，能够实时可视化射频场的空间分布、路径延迟和极化特性，所有显示数据均直接源自实际测量的信道数据。

Conclusion: 该增强现实可视化系统为科学传播、教学和实验调试提供了一种直观的工具，通过将抽象的射频现象转化为可视化的实时图像，降低了理解无线通信原理的门槛，同时保证了数据的真实性和可追溯性。

Abstract: Wireless multipath propagation, beamforming, and polarization are central concepts in radio systems, but they are difficult to observe directly because radio-frequency fields are invisible to humans. This paper presents an augmented-reality visualization system that turns phase-coherent WiFi channel measurements from the ESPARGOS antenna array into a live camera overlay. The system estimates a two-dimensional beamspace representation, registers it with the optical camera view, and overlays the resulting angular spectrum onto the physical scene. Additional visual layers show relative path delay and polarization, the latter computed using Jones calculus from channel measurements captured from two separate antenna feeds. The result is an intuitive ``WiFi camera'' for science communication, teaching, and experimental debugging, while all displayed quantities remain directly derived from measured channel data.

</details>


### [173] [CardioFusion-AI: Robust ECG--PPG Fusion for Multimodal Physiological Monitoring Under Signal Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26000)
*Navaneetha Krishnan Kamalakannan,Janakiraman Kamalakannan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: CardioFusion-AI提出了一种自适应融合框架，用于处理可穿戴ECG和PPG传感器在不同退化条件下的互补但脆弱的特性。研究发现模态可用性与模态质量是自适应融合中功能上不同的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴ECG和PPG传感器虽然互补，但各自易受运动伪影、接触不良和传感器丢失的影响而退化。传统的融合策略假设两种模态同等可信，在退化条件下可能比单一清洁模态更不可靠。

Method: 开发CardioFusion-AI框架，包含信号处理前端（R峰和收缩期峰检测、Orphanidou型信号质量指数、逐拍脉搏传输时间估计）。在53个真实ICU记录（848个窗口）和真实标注胎儿ECG数据库上验证。进行受控合成退化研究，比较8种ECG-PPG融合策略在6种退化条件下（涵盖分级腐败和完全模态丢失）的表现，使用5个独立训练种子。

Result: ECG心率平均绝对误差1.61 bpm，PPG为2.78 bpm；R峰F1值0.89-0.98。注意力融合在描述性总体误差方面最低（1.66±0.43 bpm）。自适应门在完全模态丢失时重新分配权重至健康模态，但在分级退化下门权重与信号质量相关性接近零（r = 0.10-0.24）。信号质量条件化在缺失PPG条件下取得特定改善（1.56±0.59 bpm），接近1.48 bpm的单模态上限。由于仅有5个训练种子，无配对比较通过Holm校正显著性检验。

Conclusion: 结果表明，模态可用性和模态质量是自适应融合中功能上不同的问题，需要分别处理。

Abstract: Wearable electrocardiogram (ECG) and photoplethysmogram (PPG) sensors are complementary but individually fragile: motion artifact, poor contact, and sensor dropout can degrade one or both signals. Fusion strategies that assume both modalities are equally trustworthy can become less reliable than a single clean modality under degradation. We present CardioFusion-AI, a framework whose signal-processing front end, including R-peak and systolic-peak detection, an Orphanidou-type signal-quality index, and beat-by-beat pulse transit time estimation, is validated on 53 real intensive-care recordings (848 windows; heart-rate mean absolute error 1.61 bpm for ECG and 2.78 bpm for PPG) and a real annotated fetal ECG database (R-peak F1 0.89-0.98). We then conduct a controlled synthetic degradation study comparing eight ECG-PPG fusion strategies across six degradation regimes spanning graded corruption and complete modality loss, using five independent training seeds. Attention fusion achieved the lowest descriptive overall error (1.66+/-0.43 bpm). Both adaptive gates reallocated weight toward the healthy modality under complete modality loss, but showed near-zero correlation between gate weight and signal quality under graded degradation (r = 0.10-0.24). Signal-quality conditioning produced a specific improvement under missing-PPG conditions (1.56+/-0.59 bpm), approaching the 1.48 bpm unimodal ceiling. With only five training seeds, no pairwise comparison survives Holm-corrected significance testing; effect sizes and confidence intervals are therefore reported. These results indicate that modality availability and modality quality are functionally distinct problems for adaptive fusion.

</details>


### [174] [Adaptive Peer Clustering with Hierarchical Random Linear Network Coding for Resilient Decentralized Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26040)
*Navaneetha Krishnan Kamalakannan,Harinisri Velmurugan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出APC-RLNC，通过自适应对等聚类与分层RLNC提升去中心化无线网络在动态拓扑和异构链路质量下的通信健壮性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化无线网络（如车联网集群、物联网节点群、边缘AI网络）需要能在动态拓扑和异构链路条件下保持鲁棒的通信协议；随机线性网络编码（RLNC）虽可提供代数抗丢包能力，但在节点信道条件差异较大时性能显著下降。

Method: 提出APC-RLNC系统：基于指数加权移动平均（EWMA）可靠性指标动态对等节点聚类，并在集群内部及跨集群应用多层网络编码；形式化聚类优化问题，推导马尔可夫丢包信道下的解码概率闭式界，并在跟随正则化领导者（FTRL）框架下证明在线重配置的O(√T)遗憾界。

Result: 仿真与Jetson Nano测试床实验表明，在高移动性车联网、突发错误信道和对抗干扰等场景中，与最先进基线相比，包交付率提升5.2–9.8个百分点，延迟降低10–23%，节点保持率提高最高30%；系统可线性扩展至500+节点，实时重配置开销低于3%。

Conclusion: APC-RLNC将自适应聚类确立为AI原生6G无线系统的基础原语，显著增强去中心化无线网络的通信鲁棒性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Decentralized wireless collectives including vehicular swarms, IoT clusters, and edge AI networks require communication protocols that maintain robustness under dynamic topologies and heterogeneous link quality. While Random Linear Network Coding (RLNC) provides algebraic resilience against packet erasures, its performance degrades significantly when peers exhibit diverse channel conditions. This paper presents Adaptive Peer Clustering with Hierarchical RLNC (APC-RLNC), a system that dynamically groups peers by exponentially weighted moving average (EWMA) reliability metrics and applies multi-tier network coding within and across clusters. We formalize the clustering optimization problem, derive closed-form decoding probability bounds for Markov erasure channels, and prove O(sqrt(T)) regret for online reconfiguration under the Follow-the-Regularized-Leader (FTRL) framework. Our implementation includes both a high-fidelity network simulator and a proof-of-concept testbed deployment on Jetson Nano edge devices. Evaluation across diverse scenarios including high-mobility vehicular networks, burst-error channels, and adversarial interference demonstrates 5.2-9.8 percentage-point packet delivery ratio (PDR) improvements, 10-23% latency reductions, and up to 30% higher node retention compared to state-of-the-art baselines. The system exhibits linear scalability to 500+ nodes and maintains real-time reconfiguration overhead below 3%. APC-RLNC establishes adaptive clustering as a foundational primitive for AI-native 6G wireless systems.

</details>


### [175] [Rethinking the Foundations of Two-Sided AI Models for 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22918)
*Yongjeong Oh,Zihan Chen,Timothy J. O'Shea,Junyong Shin,Jinho Choi,Yo-Seb Jeon,Jihong Park*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对下一代空口双侧AI模型实际部署面临的三大挑战（与遗留用户隔离、预定义信道条件训练、需大量跨厂商通信的梯度微调），提出三项实用化解决方案：将双侧模型集成至5G NR协议栈实现共存；构建紧凑模型表实现高效信道适应；提出仅需标量反馈的无梯度零阶微调促进多厂商互操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双侧AI模型研究存在三个不切实际的假设：1)与遗留用户完全隔离；2)需在大量预定义信道条件下训练；3)基于梯度的微调需要大量跨厂商通信。这些假设严重阻碍了双侧AI模型在实际5G/6G网络中的部署。

Method: 1.协议栈集成方法：将双侧模型处理嵌入5G NR协议栈，并在真实测试床上验证与传统NR的共存操作；2.紧凑模型表方法：联合优化双侧模型与可训练代理信道，根据当前信道条件选择最优模型；3.无梯度零阶微调方法：仅使用标量反馈替代传统梯度向量交换，降低跨厂商通信开销并保护模型隐私。

Result: 1.验证了双侧AI模型可在真实5G NR测试床上与传统NR共存操作；2.实现了高任务性能与低训练/存储开销的信道自适应，无需海量预定义信道条件训练；3.达成仅需标量反馈的微调方案，显著减少跨厂商通信量并增强隐私保护。

Conclusion: 本文提出的三项实用化方案有效推进了双侧AI模型从理论到实际部署的转变，解决了遗留系统共存、信道适应和跨厂商互操作等关键工程挑战，但同时也揭示了标准化、实时性优化和安全性等开放问题仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: For next-generation air interfaces, two-sided artificial intelligence (AI) models have received growing attention, with AI models deployed at both the transmitter and receiver for efficient channel feedback and data communication. However, their practical deployment is complicated by assumptions commonly made in existing studies, including isolation from legacy users, training under predefined channel conditions, and gradient-based fine-tuning requiring substantial cross-vendor communication. This article revisits these assumptions and presents practical alternatives. First, for legacy coexistence, we integrate two-sided model processing into the 5G New Radio (NR) protocol stack and validate its operation alongside conventional NR on a real-world testbed. Second, instead of training under a massive number of predefined channel conditions, we construct a compact model table by jointly optimizing two-sided models with trainable surrogate channels, and select the best model according to the current channel condition to enable channel adaptation with high task performance and low training/storage overhead. Finally, unlike existing fine-tuning that exchanges large gradient vectors containing potentially private model information, we present gradient-free zeroth-order fine-tuning that requires only scalar feedback, facilitating multi-vendor interoperability. Together, these approaches advance the practical deployment of two-sided AI models while highlighting key open challenges.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [VLM-based automatic multi-granularity graph representation of building layouts for design informatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24886)
*Song Guo,Zhuoshi Chen,Maosu Li,Weimin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 利用视觉语言模型自动构建多层级图表示(LoG)，从建筑平面图图像中提取结构化的空间关系知识，实现无标注的布局信息提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑平面图编码了丰富的功能性空间关系知识，对设计检索、基于知识的推理和BIM全生命周期增强至关重要。然而，为公共建筑自动构建任务自适应的图表示仍具挑战性。

Method: 提出基于视觉语言模型(VLM)的自动LoG构建方法，包括节点识别、边推断、文本解析和图粗化处理。定义多层级图(Level-of-Graphs)表示，并在147个全球学术图书馆平面图上进行实证评估。

Result: VLM生成的图与人工标注图高度一致(节点匹配率≥92%)，每个平面图三层级图生成用时509.3秒。中粒度图在节点级区域预测方面表现最佳(Macro F1=0.647，复杂度仅为细粒度65%)，粗粒度图在图级布局质量评估中效果最佳(Spearman相关系数=0.610，复杂度仅为细粒度16%)。

Conclusion: 通过实现从平面图图像中可缩放、无标注地提取结构化布局信息，本研究通过将平面图转换为知识表示，推进了设计信息学发展，从而增强了建筑全生命周期中设计信息的利用效率。

Abstract: Architectural floorplan images encode rich relational knowledge among functional spaces, which underpins design retrieval, knowledge-based reasoning, and BIM enrichment through the building lifecycle. However, it remains challenging to automatically construct task-adaptive graph representations for public buildings. To address this gap, we first define a multi-granularity Level-of-Graphs (LoGs) for public building layouts. Methodologically, we present a Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based automatic LoG construction through node identification, edge inference, text parsing, and graph coarsening. VLM-generated representations are systematically evaluated and tested in real-world tasks, using 147 academic library floorplans worldwide as a case study. Experiments showed VLM-generated graphs were broadly consistent with human-labeled graphs (matched node ratio >= 92%; 509.3 s per floor plan for three-LoG graph generation). Meso-grained graphs yield the best node-level zone prediction (Macro F1 = 0.647, at 65% of fine-grained complexity), while coarse-grained graphs are most effective for graph-level layout quality evaluation (Spearman's \r{ho} = 0.610, at 16% of fine-grained complexity). By enabling scalable, annotation-free extraction of structured layout information from floorplan images, this study advances design informatics by converting plan images into knowledge representations, thereby enhancing the utilization of design information across the building life cycle.

</details>


### [177] [SIMGUIDE: Procedurally Grounded Multi-Context Representations for Personalized Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24888)
*Chirag Shah*

Main category: cs.AI
